0002:   Pool,
0003: } from 'pg';

0005: type Arguments = {
0006:   actorId: string;
0007:   staffCode: string;
0008:   displayName: string;
0009:   employmentReference: string | null;
0010: };
0011: 
0012: function value(
0013:   name: string,
0014: ): string | null {
0015:   const prefix = `--${name}=`;
0016: 
0017:   const item =
0018:     process.argv
0019:       .slice(2)
0020:       .find((entry) =>
0021:         entry.startsWith(prefix),
0022:       );
0023: 
0024:   if (!item) {
0025:     return null;
0026:   }
0027: 
0028:   return item.slice(prefix.length).trim();
0029: }
0030: 
0031: function required(
0032:   name: string,
0033: ): string {
0034:   const result = value(name);
0035: 
0036:   if (!result) {
0037:     throw new Error(
0038:       `Missing required --${name}=...`,
0039:     );
0040:   }
0041: 
0042:   return result;
0043: }
0044: 
0045: function parseArguments(): Arguments {
0046:   const actorId = required('actor-id');
0047:   const staffCode = required('staff-code');
0048:   const displayName = required('display-name');
0049: 
0050:   if (actorId === 'v79-phase1d-supervisor') {
0051:     throw new Error(
0052:       'Reserved historical fixture actor ID is forbidden',
0053:     );
0054:   }
0055: 
0056:   if (staffCode === 'V79-P1D-SUP-001') {
0057:     throw new Error(
0058:       'Reserved historical fixture staff code is forbidden',
0059:     );
0060:   }
0061: 
0062:   return {
0063:     actorId,
0064:     staffCode,
0065:     displayName,
0066:     employmentReference:
0067:       value('employment-reference'),
0068:   };
0069: }

0071: async function main(): Promise<void> {
0072:   const databaseUrl =
0073:     String(
0074:       process.env.DATABASE_URL || '',
0075:     ).trim();
0076: 
0077:   if (!databaseUrl) {
0078:     throw new Error(
0079:       'DATABASE_URL must be explicitly supplied',
0080:     );
0081:   }
0082: 
0083:   const args = parseArguments();
0084: 
0085:   const pool = new Pool({
0086:     connectionString: databaseUrl,
0087:   });
0088: 
0089:   const client = await pool.connect();
0090: 
0091:   try {
0092:     await client.query('BEGIN');
0093: 
0094:     await client.query(
0095:       'LOCK TABLE staff_actors IN EXCLUSIVE MODE',
0096:     );
0097: 
0098:     const baseline =
0099:       await client.query<{
0100:         staff_count: string;
0101:         active_supervisor_count: string;
0102:       }>(
0103:         `
0104:         SELECT
0105:           COUNT(*)::text AS staff_count,
0106:           COUNT(*) FILTER (
0107:             WHERE
0108:               primary_operational_role = 'SUPERVISOR'
0109:               AND status = 'ACTIVE'
0110:           )::text AS active_supervisor_count
0111:         FROM staff_actors
0112:         `,
0113:       );
0114: 
0115:     const row = baseline.rows[0];
0116: 
0117:     if (
0118:       !row
0119:       || Number(row.staff_count) !== 0
0120:       || Number(
0121:         row.active_supervisor_count,
0122:       ) !== 0
0123:     ) {
0124:       throw new Error(
0125:         'Bootstrap allowed only when staff actor count and active Supervisor count are both zero',
0126:       );
0127:     }
0128: 
0129:     const created =
0130:       await client.query(
0131:         `
0132:         WITH inserted AS (
0133:           INSERT INTO staff_actors (
0134:             actor_id,
0135:             staff_code,
0136:             display_name,
0137:             primary_operational_role,
0138:             status,
0139:             employment_reference
0140:           )
0141:           VALUES (
0142:             $1,
0143:             $2,
0144:             $3,
0145:             'SUPERVISOR',
0146:             'ACTIVE',
0147:             $4
0148:           )
0149:           RETURNING *
0150:         ),
0151:         audit_insert AS (
0152:           INSERT INTO staff_actor_audit (
0153:             event_type,
0154:             target_actor_id,
0155:             performed_by,
0156:             performed_by_role,
0157:             previous_value,
0158:             new_value
0159:           )
0160:           SELECT
0161:             'STAFF_BOOTSTRAPPED',
0162:             actor_id,
0163:             'SYSTEM_OPERATOR',
0164:             'BOOTSTRAP_OPERATOR',
0165:             NULL,
0166:             to_jsonb(inserted)
0167:           FROM inserted
0168:           RETURNING audit_id
0169:         )
0170:         SELECT
0171:           actor_id,
0172:           staff_code,
0173:           display_name,
0174:           primary_operational_role,
0175:           status,
0176:           employment_reference,
0177:           created_at,
0178:           updated_at
0179:         FROM inserted
0180:         `,
0181:         [
0182:           args.actorId,
0183:           args.staffCode,
0184:           args.displayName,
0185:           args.employmentReference,
0186:         ],
0187:       );
0188: 
0189:     if (created.rowCount !== 1) {
0190:       throw new Error(
0191:         'Bootstrap did not create exactly one Supervisor',
0192:       );
0193:     }
0194: 
0195:     await client.query('COMMIT');
0196: 
0197:     console.log(
0198:       'STAFF_BOOTSTRAP_STATUS=PASSED',
0199:     );
0200: 
0201:     console.log(
0202:       'BOOTSTRAPPED_ACTOR_ID='
0203:       + args.actorId,
0204:     );
0205:

0210:     console.log(
0211:       'BOOTSTRAPPED_STATUS=ACTIVE',
0212:     );
0213:   } catch (error) {
0214:     await client.query('ROLLBACK');
0215:     throw error;
0216:   } finally {

0218:     await pool.end();
0219:   }

FILE=/Users/anhha/Downloads/TamAnCare_V8_0_Full_Demo/api/src/activity-rehabilitation/activity-rehabilitation-authorization.service.ts
0001: import {
0002:   BadRequestException,
0003:   Injectable,
0004: } from '@nestjs/common';
0005: 
0006: import {
0007:   RehabActorRole,
0008:   RehabCommand,
0009: } from './activity-rehabilitation.types';
0010: 
0011: @Injectable()
0012: export class ActivityRehabilitationAuthorizationService {
0013:   private normalizeRole(role: RehabActorRole | string): RehabActorRole {
0014:     return String(role ?? '')
0015:       .trim()
0016:       .toUpperCase() as RehabActorRole;
0017:   }
0018: 
0019:   assertHuman(command: RehabCommand): void {
0020:     const role = this.normalizeRole(command.actorRole);
0021: 
0022:     if (role === 'AI' || role === 'SYSTEM') {
0023:       throw new BadRequestException(
0024:         'AI or SYSTEM cannot perform official rehabilitation mutation.',
0025:       );
0026:     }
0027:   }
0028: 
0029:   assertManager(command: RehabCommand): void {
0030:     this.assertHuman(command);
0031: 
0032:     const role = this.normalizeRole(command.actorRole);
0033: 
0034:     if (!['SUPERVISOR', 'CARE_MANAGER'].includes(role)) {
0035:       throw new BadRequestException(
0036:         'Only SUPERVISOR or CARE_MANAGER is authorized.',
0037:       );
0038:     }
0039:   }
0040: 
0041:   assertClinicalHuman(command: RehabCommand): void {
0042:     this.assertHuman(command);
0043: 
0044:     const role = this.normalizeRole(command.actorRole);
0045: 
0046:     if (!['NURSE', 'SUPERVISOR', 'CARE_MANAGER'].includes(role)) {
0047:       throw new BadRequestException(
0048:         'Authorized human clinical reviewer required.',
0049:       );
0050:     }
0051:   }
0052: 
0053:   assertSessionCreator(command: RehabCommand): void {
0054:     this.assertClinicalHuman(command);
0055:   }
0056: 
0057:   assertAssignedOwner(
0058:     command: RehabCommand,
0059:     assignedTo: string | null,
0060:   ): void {
0061:     this.assertHuman(command);
0062: 
0063:     if (!assignedTo || assignedTo !== command.actorId) {
0064:       throw new BadRequestException(
0065:         'Only the assigned human owner may perform this transition.',
0066:       );
0067:     }
0068:   }
0069: 
0070:   assertReviewer(
0071:     command: RehabCommand,
0072:     reviewer: string | null,
0073:   ): void {
0074:     this.assertHuman(command);
0075: 
0076:     if (!reviewer || reviewer !== command.actorId) {
0077:       throw new BadRequestException(
0078:         'Only the assigned human reviewer may perform this transition.',
0079:       );
0080:     }
0081:   }
0082: }

FILE=/Users/anhha/Downloads/TamAnCare_V8_0_Full_Demo/api/src/auth/auth.controller.ts
0001: import {
0002:   Body,
0003:   Controller,
0004:   Headers,
0005:   HttpCode,
0006:   Post,
0007:   UnauthorizedException,
0008: } from '@nestjs/common';
0009: 
0010: import {
0011:   AuthService,
0012: } from './auth.service';
0013: 
0014: @Controller('api/auth')
0015: export class AuthController {
0016:   constructor(
0017:     private readonly authService:
0018:       AuthService,
0019:   ) {}
0020: 
0021:   @Post('login')
0022:   @HttpCode(200)
0023:   login(
0024:     @Body()
0025:     body: {
0026:       actorId?: string;
0027:       password?: string;
0028:     },
0029:   ) {
0030:     return this.authService.login(
0031:       String(body.actorId || ''),
0032:       String(body.password || ''),
0033:     );
0034:   }
0035: 
0036:   @Post('logout')
0037:   @HttpCode(204)
0038:   async logout(
0039:     @Headers('x-actor-id')
0040:     actorId?: string,
0041: 
0042:     @Headers('x-auth-session-id')
0043:     sessionId?: string,
0044:   ): Promise<void> {
0045:     if (!actorId || !sessionId) {
0046:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0047:         'Authentication session is required',
0048:       );
0049:     }
0050: 
0051:     await this.authService.revokeSession(
0052:       actorId,
0053:       sessionId,
0054:     );
0055:   }
0056: }

FILE=/Users/anhha/Downloads/TamAnCare_V8_0_Full_Demo/api/src/auth/auth.service.ts
0001: import {
0002:   Injectable,
0003:   UnauthorizedException,
0004: } from '@nestjs/common';
0005: 
0006: import {
0007:   createHmac,
0008:   pbkdf2Sync,
0009:   randomUUID,
0010:   timingSafeEqual,
0011: } from 'crypto';
0012: 
0013: import {
0014:   DatabaseService,
0015: } from '../database/database.service';
0016: 
0017: interface CredentialRow {
0018:   actor_id: string;
0019:   password_hash: string;
0020:   password_salt: string;
0021:   password_iterations: number;
0022:   password_digest: string;
0023:   locked_until: Date | string | null;
0024:   primary_operational_role: string;
0025:   status: string;
0026: }
0027: 
0028: @Injectable()
0029: export class AuthService {
0030:   constructor(
0031:     private readonly db:
0032:       DatabaseService,
0033:   ) {}
0034: 
0035:   async login(
0036:     actorId: string,
0037:     password: string,
0038:   ) {
0039:     if (!actorId || !password) {
0040:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0041:         'Invalid credentials',
0042:       );
0043:     }
0044: 
0045:     const result =
0046:       await this.db.query<CredentialRow>(
0047:         `
0048:         SELECT
0049:           c.actor_id,
0050:           c.password_hash,
0051:           c.password_salt,
0052:           c.password_iterations,
0053:           c.password_digest,
0054:           c.locked_until,
0055:           s.primary_operational_role,
0056:           s.status
0057:         FROM auth_credentials c
0058:         JOIN staff_actors s
0059:           ON s.actor_id = c.actor_id
0060:         WHERE c.actor_id = $1
0061:         LIMIT 1
0062:         `,
0063:         [actorId],
0064:       );
0065: 
0066:     const row = result.rows[0];
0067: 
0068:     if (
0069:       !row ||
0070:       row.status !== 'ACTIVE' ||
0071:       (
0072:         row.locked_until &&
0073:         new Date(
0074:           row.locked_until,
0075:         ).getTime() > Date.now()
0076:       )
0077:     ) {
0078:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0079:         'Invalid credentials',
0080:       );
0081:     }
0082: 
0083:     const calculated =
0084:       pbkdf2Sync(
0085:         password,
0086:         row.password_salt,
0087:         row.password_iterations,
0088:         32,
0089:         row.password_digest,
0090:       );
0091: 
0092:     const expected =
0093:       Buffer.from(
0094:         row.password_hash,
0095:         'hex',
0096:       );
0097: 
0098:     const valid =
0099:       expected.length ===
0100:         calculated.length &&
0101:       timingSafeEqual(
0102:         expected,
0103:         calculated,
0104:       );
0105: 
0106:     if (!valid) {
0107:       await this.db.query(
0108:         `
0109:         UPDATE auth_credentials
0110:         SET
0111:           failed_attempts =
0112:             failed_attempts + 1,
0113:           locked_until =
0114:             CASE
0115:               WHEN failed_attempts + 1 >= 5
0116:               THEN now() + interval '15 minutes'
0117:               ELSE locked_until
0118:             END,
0119:           updated_at = now()
0120:         WHERE actor_id = $1
0121:         `,
0122:         [actorId],
0123:       );
0124: 
0125:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0126:         'Invalid credentials',
0127:       );
0128:     }
0129: 
0130:     await this.db.query(
0131:       `
0132:       UPDATE auth_credentials
0133:       SET
0134:         failed_attempts = 0,
0135:         locked_until = NULL,
0136:         last_login_at = now(),
0137:         updated_at = now()
0138:       WHERE actor_id = $1
0139:       `,
0140:       [actorId],
0141:     );
0142: 
0143:     const now =
0144:       Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
0145: 
0146:     const expiresAt =
0147:       now + 3600;
0148: 
0149:     const sessionId =
0150:       randomUUID();
0151: 
0152:     await this.db.query(
0153:       `
0154:       INSERT INTO auth_sessions (
0155:         session_id,
0156:         actor_id,
0157:         actor_role,
0158:         issued_at,
0159:         expires_at
0160:       )
0161:       VALUES (
0162:         $1,
0163:         $2,
0164:         $3,
0165:         to_timestamp($4),
0166:         to_timestamp($5)
0167:       )
0168:       `,
0169:       [
0170:         sessionId,
0171:         row.actor_id,
0172:         row.primary_operational_role,
0173:         now,
0174:         expiresAt,
0175:       ],
0176:     );
0177: 
0178:     return {
0179:       accessToken:
0180:         this.issueToken(
0181:           row.actor_id,
0182:           row.primary_operational_role,
0183:           sessionId,
0184:           now,
0185:           expiresAt,
0186:         ),
0187:       tokenType: 'Bearer',
0188:       expiresIn: 3600,
0189:     };
0190:   }
0191: 
0192:   async revokeSession(
0193:     actorId: string,
0194:     sessionId: string,
0195:   ): Promise<void> {
0196:     if (!actorId || !sessionId) {
0197:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0198:         'Invalid authentication session',
0199:       );
0200:     }
0201: 
0202:     const result =
0203:       await this.db.query(
0204:         `
0205:         UPDATE auth_sessions
0206:         SET
0207:           revoked_at = now(),
0208:           revoked_reason = 'LOGOUT'
0209:         WHERE session_id = $1
0210:           AND actor_id = $2
0211:           AND revoked_at IS NULL
0212:         `,
0213:         [
0214:           sessionId,
0215:           actorId,
0216:         ],
0217:       );
0218: 
0219:     if (result.rowCount !== 1) {
0220:       throw new UnauthorizedException(
0221:         'Authentication session is not active',
0222:       );
0223:     }
0224:   }
0225: 
0226:   private issueToken(
0227:     actorId: string,
0228:     role: string,
0229:     sessionId: string,
0230:     now: number,
0231:     expiresAt: number,
0232:   ): string {
0233:     const secret =
0234:       process.env.JWT_SECRET;
0235: 
0236:     if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
0237:       throw new Error(
0238:         'JWT_SECRET must contain at least 32 characters',
0239:       );
0240:     }
0241: 
0242:     const encode =
0243:       (value: unknown) =>
0244:         Buffer
0245:           .from(
0246:             JSON.stringify(value),
0247:           )
0248:           .toString('base64url');
0249: 
0250:     const unsigned =
0251:       `${encode({
0252:         alg: 'HS256',
0253:         typ: 'JWT',
0254:       })}.${encode({
0255:         sub: actorId,
0256:         role,
0257:         jti: sessionId,
0258:         iat: now,
0259:         exp: expiresAt,
0260:       })}`;
0261: 
0262:     const signature =
0263:       createHmac(
0264:         'sha256',
0265:         secret,
0266:       )
0267:         .update(unsigned)
0268:         .digest('base64url');
0269: 
0270:     return `${unsigned}.${signature}`;
0271:   }
0272: }

0012: export class CarePlanAuthorizationService {
0013: 
0014:   authorize(
0015:     input: CarePlanAuthorizationInput,
0016:   ): CarePlanAuthorizationDecision {
0017: 
0018:     const status =
0019:       String(
0020:         input.currentStatus ?? '',
0021:       )
0022:         .trim()
0023:         .toUpperCase();
0024: 
0025:     const actorId =
0026:       String(
0027:         input.actorId ?? '',
0028:       ).trim();
0029: 
0030:     const actorRole =
0031:       String(
0032:         input.actorRole ?? '',
0033:       )
0034:         .trim()
0035:         .toUpperCase();
0036: 
0037:     const action =
0038:       input.action;
0039: 
0040:     if (!actorId) {
0041:       throw new Error(
0042:         'actorId is required.',
0043:       );
0044:     }
0045: 
0046:     if (!actorRole) {
0047:       throw new Error(
0048:         'actorRole is required.',
0049:       );
0050:     }
0051: 
0052:     if (
0053:       actorRole === 'AI' ||
0054:       actorRole === 'SYSTEM'
0055:     ) {
0056:       throw new Error(
0057:         'AI or SYSTEM actor cannot govern a Care Plan.',
0058:       );
0059:     }
0060: 
0061:     if (
0062:       actorRole !== 'SUPERVISOR' &&
0063:       actorRole !== 'CARE_MANAGER'
0064:     ) {
0065:       throw new Error(
0066:         'Actor is not authorized to govern this Care Plan.',
0067:       );
0068:     }
0069: 
0070:     if (
0071:       action === 'ACTIVATE'
0072:     ) {
0073:       if (status !== 'DRAFT') {
0074:         throw new Error(
0075:           'Only a DRAFT Care Plan can be activated.',
0076:         );
0077:       }
0078: 
0079:       return {
0080:         authorized: true,
0081:         actorId,
0082:         actorRole,
0083:         action,
0084:         targetStatus:
0085:           'ACTIVE',
0086:         auditEvent:
0087:           'PLAN_ACTIVATED',
0088:         autonomousClinicalAction:
0089:           false,
0090:       };
0091:     }
0092: 
0093:     if (
0094:       action === 'REACTIVATE'
0095:     ) {
0096:       if (
0097:         status !==
0098:         'SUSPENDED'
0099:       ) {
0100:         throw new Error(
0101:           'Only a SUSPENDED Care Plan can be reactivated.',
0102:         );
0103:       }
0104: 
0105:       return {
0106:         authorized: true,
0107:         actorId,
0108:         actorRole,
0109:         action,
0110:         targetStatus:
0111:           'ACTIVE',
0112:         auditEvent:
0113:           'PLAN_REACTIVATED',
0114:         autonomousClinicalAction:
0115:           false,
0116:       };
0117:     }
0118: 
0119:     if (
0120:       action === 'SUSPEND'
0121:     ) {
0122:       if (
0123:         status !==
0124:         'ACTIVE'
0125:       ) {
0126:         throw new Error(
0127:           'Only an ACTIVE Care Plan can be suspended.',
0128:         );
0129:       }
0130: 
0131:       return {
0132:         authorized: true,
0133:         actorId,
0134:         actorRole,
0135:         action,
0136:         targetStatus:
0137:           'SUSPENDED',
0138:         auditEvent:
0139:           'PLAN_SUSPENDED',
0140:         autonomousClinicalAction:
0141:           false,
0142:       };
0143:     }
0144: 
0145:     if (
0146:       action === 'COMPLETE'
0147:     ) {
0148:       if (
0149:         status !==
0150:         'ACTIVE'
0151:       ) {
0152:         throw new Error(
0153:           'Only an ACTIVE Care Plan can be completed.',
0154:         );
0155:       }
0156: 
0157:       return {
0158:         authorized: true,
0159:         actorId,
0160:         actorRole,
0161:         action,
0162:         targetStatus:
0163:           'COMPLETED',
0164:         auditEvent:
0165:           'PLAN_COMPLETED',
0166:         autonomousClinicalAction:
0167:           false,
0168:       };
0169:     }
0170: 
0171:     if (
0172:       action === 'CANCEL'
0173:     ) {
0174:       if (
0175:         status !== 'DRAFT' &&
0176:         status !== 'ACTIVE' &&
0177:         status !== 'SUSPENDED'
0178:       ) {
0179:         throw new Error(
0180:           'Only a DRAFT, ACTIVE, or SUSPENDED Care Plan can be cancelled.',
0181:         );
0182:       }
0183: 
0184:       return {
0185:         authorized: true,
0186:         actorId,
0187:         actorRole,
0188:         action,
0189:         targetStatus:
0190:           'CANCELLED',
0191:         auditEvent:
0192:           'PLAN_CANCELLED',
0193:         autonomousClinicalAction:
0194:           false,
0195:       };
0196:     }
0197: 
0198:     throw new Error(
0199:       'Unsupported Care Plan governance action.',
0200:     );
0201:   }
0202: }

0047:       throw new Error(
0048:         'actorRole is required.',

0054:       role === 'SYSTEM'

0097:       actor.actorRole;

FILE=/Users/anhha/Downloads/TamAnCare_V8_0_Full_Demo/api/src/inventory/inventory-authority.service.ts
0001: import {
0002:   ForbiddenException,
0003:   Injectable,
0004:   UnauthorizedException,
0005: } from '@nestjs/common';
0006: import { PoolClient } from 'pg';
0007: import { DatabaseService } from '../database/database.service';
0008: 
0009: export type InventoryHumanRole =
0010:   | 'CAREGIVER'
0011:   | 'NURSE'
0012:   | 'CARE_MANAGER'
0013:   | 'SUPERVISOR';
0014: 
0015: export interface InventoryActor {
0016:   actorId: string;
0017:   actorRole: InventoryHumanRole;
0018: }
0019: 
0020: @Injectable()
0021: export class InventoryAuthorityService {
0022:   private readonly humanRoles: InventoryHumanRole[] = [
0023:     'CAREGIVER',
0024:     'NURSE',
0025:     'CARE_MANAGER',
0026:     'SUPERVISOR',
0027:   ];
0028: 
0029:   private readonly authorityRoles: InventoryHumanRole[] = [
0030:     'CARE_MANAGER',
0031:     'SUPERVISOR',
0032:   ];
0033: 
0034:   constructor(private readonly database: DatabaseService) {}
0035: 
0036:   async requireActor(
0037:     actorId?: string,
0038:     actorRole?: string,
0039:     client?: PoolClient,
0040:   ): Promise<InventoryActor> {
0041:     const id = String(actorId || '').trim();
0042:     const role =
0043:       String(actorRole || '').trim().toUpperCase() as InventoryHumanRole;
0044: 
0045:     if (!id || !role) {
0046:       throw new UnauthorizedException('Actor context is required');
0047:     }
0048: 
0049:     if (!this.humanRoles.includes(role)) {
0050:       throw new ForbiddenException('Human operational role is required');
0051:     }
0052: 
0053:     const query = client
0054:       ? client.query.bind(client)
0055:       : this.database.query.bind(this.database);
0056: 
0057:     const result = await query(
0058:       `
0059:       SELECT actor_id, primary_operational_role, status
0060:       FROM staff_actors
0061:       WHERE actor_id = $1
0062:       LIMIT 1
0063:       `,
0064:       [id],
0065:     );
0066: 
0067:     if (!result.rowCount) {
0068:       throw new UnauthorizedException('Canonical actor not found');
0069:     }
0070: 
0071:     const actor: any = result.rows[0];
0072: 
0073:     if (actor.status !== 'ACTIVE') {
0074:       throw new ForbiddenException('Canonical actor is not active');
0075:     }
0076: 
0077:     if (actor.primary_operational_role !== role) {
0078:       throw new ForbiddenException(
0079:         'Actor role does not match canonical role',
0080:       );
0081:     }
0082: 
0083:     return {
0084:       actorId: actor.actor_id,
0085:       actorRole: actor.primary_operational_role,
0086:     };
0087:   }
0088: 
0089:   requireAuthority(actor: InventoryActor): void {
0090:     if (!this.authorityRoles.includes(actor.actorRole)) {
0091:       throw new ForbiddenException(
0092:         'Care manager or supervisor authority is required',
0093:       );
0094:     }
0095:   }
0096: }

0065:     }

0079:       throw new Error(

0081:       );
0082:     }
0083:

0099:         !== 'AI_ALERT'

0101:         throw new Error(
0102:           'AI may create suspected alert only as AI_ALERT.',
0103:         );
0104:       }
0105: 
0106:       return actor;
0107:     }
0108: 
0109:     return this.requireHuman(input);
0110:   }
0111: }

FILE=/Users/anhha/Downloads/TamAnCare_V8_0_Full_Demo/api/src/start-review-authorization/start-review-authorization.service.ts
0001: import {
0002:   Injectable,
0003: } from '@nestjs/common';
0004: 
0005: import {
0006:   StartReviewAuthorizationInput,
0007:   StartReviewAuthorizationResult,
0008: } from './start-review-authorization.types';
0009: 
0010: @Injectable()
0011: export class StartReviewAuthorizationService {
0012: 
0013:   private readonly supervisorRoles =
0014:     new Set<string>([
0015:       'SUPERVISOR',
0016:       'CARE_MANAGER',
0017:     ]);
0018: 
0019: 
0020:   private requireText(
0021:     value: unknown,
0022:     name: string,
0023:   ): string {
0024: 
0025:     const normalized =
0026:       String(
0027:         value ?? '',
0028:       ).trim();
0029: 
0030:     if (!normalized) {
0031:       throw new Error(
0032:         `${name} is required.`,
0033:       );
0034:     }
0035: 
0036:     return normalized;
0037:   }
0038: 
0039: 
0040:   private normalizeRole(
0041:     value: unknown,
0042:   ): string {
0043: 
0044:     return this.requireText(
0045:       value,
0046:       'actorRole',
0047:     ).toUpperCase();
0048:   }
0049: 
0050: 
0051:   authorize(
0052:     input:
0053:       StartReviewAuthorizationInput,
0054:   ): StartReviewAuthorizationResult {
0055: 
0056:     const action =
0057:       input.action;
0058: 
0059:     if (!action) {
0060:       throw new Error(
0061:         'Care Action is required.',
0062:       );
0063:     }
0064: 
0065:     const actorId =
0066:       this.requireText(
0067:         input.actorId,
0068:         'actorId',
0069:       );
0070: 
0071:     const actorRole =
0072:       this.normalizeRole(
0073:         input.actorRole,
0074:       );
0075: 
0076:     if (
0077:       actorRole === 'AI' ||
0078:       actorRole === 'SYSTEM'
0079:     ) {
0080:       throw new Error(
0081:         'AI or SYSTEM actor cannot start Care Action review.',
0082:       );
0083:     }
0084: 
0085:     if (
0086:       action.status !==
0087:         'PENDING'
0088:     ) {
0089:       throw new Error(
0090:         'Only a PENDING Care Action can start review.',
0091:       );
0092:     }
0093: 
0094:     if (
0095:       !action.assignedTo ||
0096:       !action.assignedRole
0097:     ) {
0098:       throw new Error(
0099:         'Care Action must have an assigned human owner before review can start.',
0100:       );
0101:     }
0102: 
0103:     const isOwner =
0104:       actorId ===
0105:         action.assignedTo;
0106: 
0107:     const isSupervisor =
0108:       this.supervisorRoles
0109:         .has(
0110:           actorRole,
0111:         );
0112: 
0113:     if (
0114:       !isOwner &&
0115:       !isSupervisor
0116:     ) {
0117:       throw new Error(
0118:         'Actor is not authorized to start review for this Care Action.',
0119:       );
0120:     }
0121: 
0122:     const mode =
0123:       isOwner
0124:         ? 'OWNER' as const
0125:         : 'SUPERVISOR_OVERRIDE' as const;
0126: 
0127:     return {
0128:       authorized:
0129:         true,
0130: 
0131:       mode,
0132: 
0133:       careActionId:
0134:         action.id,
0135: 
0136:       residentId:
0137:         action.residentId,
0138: 
0139:       patternId:
0140:         action.patternId,
0141: 
0142:       ownerId:
0143:         action.assignedTo,
0144: 
0145:       ownerRole:
0146:         action.assignedRole,
0147: 
0148:       actorId,
0149: 
0150:       actorRole,
0151: 
0152:       ownershipChanged:
0153:         false,
0154: 
0155:       transferRequired:
0156:         false,
0157: 
0158:       auditEvent:
0159:         'REVIEW_STARTED',
0160: 
0161:       autonomousClinicalAction:
0162:         false,
0163:     };
0164:   }
0165: }
